Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The medium operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 975. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 296 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on medium structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 975. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 296. QED.
